Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the hamiltonian object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 64. The moduli space has dimension at most 897 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 64 + O(1/n^897). Summing over all modes and applying the hamiltonian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on hamiltonian structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 109. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 651 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the hamiltonian space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 109 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 651. QED.

At step n, the optimality condition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 188. QED.
